 ## 启动与停止
 
 在本目录双击 **Start-Capybara.cmd**。脚本检查依赖、构建游戏、启动隐藏的本地服务，并尝试打开 **http://127.0.0.1:4175/**。当前机器已经装好依赖。若系统限制自动打开浏览器，直接打开上述地址即可。
 
 双击 **Stop-Capybara.cmd** 停止由启动脚本创建的服务。重复启动会复用正在运行的本作服务；停止脚本通过专属记录及令牌识别服务，不会结束其他端口的程序。

 ## 怎么玩
 
 1. 点「开泡」，用「摇铃开饭」花固定 10 口粮抽取 5 只萌宠或组件，拖到下半场的白垫。窝里还有单位时可直接点「换一窝」：丢弃未摆放的剩余单位，支付 10 口粮重新抽 5 只；场上单位不受影响，口粮不足时保留原卡。也支持先点单位、再点目标格；折叠的精确点选区支持键盘操作。
 2. 同种同级基础宠升星。动物与对应组件双向合成英雄；相同等级同名英雄可升星，英雄也可以吃自己的任一配方散件升级。英雄最高 5 级，蜜蜂最高 3 级。
 3. 英雄合成或升级瞬间解除纸箱、淋湿、击倒和混乱；剪刀禁合必须等待结束。非合成配对交换位置，柴犬与魔法帽会触发拒戴彩蛋。
 4. 拖小铁锹到草坪，花 1 把开荒；修复二哈毁格花 2 把。开局有 2 把，每 12 秒补充 1 把，每 3 波另加 1 把，最多存 6 把。暂停会停止生产，满仓不会积压产量。
 5. 暂停可以查看双方战利品，继续战斗或投降。普通害虫越线扣 1 橘子，首领扣 2；一方橘子归零立即结束。
-6. 左侧「草坪洒水车」每局免费用一次：120 冲洗伤害、击退 4.5 格、减速 2 秒。AI 也会在敌人接近终点时使用自己的洒水车。
+6. 棋盘下方左侧「草坪洒水车」每局免费用一次：120 冲洗伤害、击退 4.5 格、减速 2 秒，并解除己方纸箱封印。AI 也会在敌人接近终点时使用自己的洒水车。
 
 开局有 20 秒摆阵；正常 1 倍速下，第一只首领在第 4 波、约 108 秒出现（开发者跳波/刷首领会提前）。首波老鼠由 58 血降至 24 血，1 级仓鼠两次普通攻击可击杀。拆家二哈每 24 秒毁一格，同半场最多同时存在 2 块坏地；修好后仍可能再次被破坏。双方使用相同的经济和产出规则。
 
 左侧测试台提供 +500 口粮、指定 19 种抽卡单位、双方刷出任意 12 首领、增减橘子、跳波、倍速与重置本作存档。还可指定 0~53 段位及四张测试地图，下局生效。测试地图是本页临时选择，不授予普通档地图解锁；主菜单选图、恢复普通档和重置会清除该临时选择。中/EN 可随时切换，包括衣帽间、黑市、名片、榜单、设置、暂停、配方和结算弹窗。
 
 12 位英雄均有实际普攻和自动大招：功夫阿宝、食铁兽教父、太极滚滚、靴子剑客喵、加特林喵、喵利波特、斩铁修勾、疾速旺财、无名刀鹅、铁山靠鸡神、拾荒浣熊王、孤寡蛙王。游戏内配方小抄展示全部配方及技能。

 - **四张地图**：阳光后院、樱花温泉馆、深夜厨房、猫爬架迷宫，均为 8×10 中心对称棋盘，每侧 16 个正交航点。历史最高段位达到 0 / 10 / 20 / 30 时永久解锁；主菜单可以跟随当前段位，也可以手选已解锁地图。厨房含 U 型半岛，迷宫含竖井与顶部走廊。
 - **12 位首领**：在原六位之上，新增策反基础宠的波斯猫、击倒宠物的拳王袋鼠、召蜂的马蜂后、混乱停火的土拨鼠、招魂骨鼠的垃圾浣熊和群体回血加速的鼠王。首领在第 4 / 6 / 8 / 9 / 10 波按主题轮换，高段位混编；新控制同样可通过合成升级立即解除，六位均接入 EMP 反噬。

+# Optional audio overrides
+
+The shipped empty `manifest.json` uses the offline, multi-track WebAudio arrangements.
+No audio files are requested unless declared. Add licensed audio beneath this directory
+and map supported keys to relative paths, for example:
+
+```json
+{"bgm_menu":"music/menu.ogg","bgm_battle":"garden.mp3","hit_soft":"hits/bubble.wav"}
+```
+
+Music keys: `bgm_menu` (84 BPM), `bgm_battle` (108 BPM), `bgm_boss` (124 BPM).
+Music files loop; export seamless loops. A successfully decoded override takes priority;
+missing files, invalid manifests and unsupported codecs quietly retain builtin audio.
+The Vite `audioManifestPlugin()` filters the dev manifest before serving it: only known
+keys with safe relative paths to existing files are returned, always as JSON with HTTP
+200. Invalid JSON and missing manifests become `{}`. The public source is never edited.
+At build completion the plugin filters `dist/audio/manifest.json` (or the configured
+in-game output directory) against the copied files. Deploy the complete build output:
+ordinary static hosts then receive no declarations for missing tracks, avoiding 404s.
+The default empty installation requests only the existing manifest. Rebuild after changing
+production audio files; deleting files after publishing requires republishing the manifest.
+
+Cue keys: `ui_click`, `summon`, `shovel`, `treasure`, `merge_upgrade`, `merge_hero`,
+`hit_soft`, `boss_spawn`, `orange_lost`, `orange_gain`, `sprinkler`, `goose`,
+`lottery_tick`, `lottery_win`, `victory`, `defeat`. Legacy `dig`, `upgrade`,
+`legendary`, `orange` are accepted; prefer canonical names so every alias uses the same
+override. If both are declared, a requested legacy cue's exact override wins.
+
+Allowed file extensions: `.mp3`, `.ogg`, `.wav`, `.m4a`, `.aac` (browser codec support
+varies). Filenames/directory segments may contain ASCII letters, digits, `_` and `-`.
+Absolute URLs, `..`, percent escapes, backslashes, query strings and fragments are rejected.
+Paths resolve relative to `audio/` under Vite's base, including subdirectory deployments.
+
+Overrides are decoded into the same AudioContext as synthesis. Music and cues share
+their respective volume sliders; overlays/hidden tabs duck music only. Hit cues remain
+globally limited to one per 95 ms. Disposal aborts loads and stops every source/loop.

+## Synchronization and loading
+
+Run `npm run sync-assets` in the game directory. The zero-dependency script copies only regular lowercase PNG filenames matching `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*\.png$` from the optional read-only source `../../design/assets/sprites/`. It scans the destination and writes a sorted array manifest. It never deletes existing destination art, creates the source, or fails because the source is absent/empty. Uppercase extensions, hyphens, directories and symlinks are skipped. Canvas and HTML share a loaded-only registry; malformed manifests, missing files and decode failures retain the built-in fallback. Vite's `BASE_URL` supports relative/subdirectory hosting. HTML refreshes once the initial asset batch settles.
+
+The lists below are the complete P1 catalog and extend the original examples above. Characters, objects, props, weapons and avatars should be transparent RGBA PNG at 256×256 or 512×512 with clear margins. Keep feet near the lower center so gold hero disks remain visible. Level/status badges, energy, hits, shields, weapon effects and input positions remain dynamic.
+
+## Four maps and tile aliases
+
+Maps fill the exact 8×10 board, 576×720 logical pixels. Recommended size: 1152×1440 or 960×1200. Align roads, grass, nests, guardian corners and the center wall to this grid. Maps may be opaque and are clipped to the board. Static G/R/S/C tiles never paint over external maps; route arrows and subtle grid lines remain.
+
+`map_sunny_backyard`, `map_sakura_onsen`, `map_midnight_kitchen`, `map_cat_tower_maze`.
+
+Dynamic tiles should be square 256×256 RGBA with transparent margins. Open tiles prefer the map-specific alias then the generic alias. Missing art becomes a translucent cream soft mat with an inset dashed border; broken tiles retain cracked rubble.
+
+`tile_open`, `tile_open_sunny_backyard`, `tile_open_sakura_onsen`, `tile_open_midnight_kitchen`, `tile_open_cat_tower_maze`, `tile_broken`.
+

+import {lstat,mkdir,readFile,realpath,stat,writeFile} from 'node:fs/promises';
+import {dirname,isAbsolute,join,relative,resolve} from 'node:path';
+import type {Plugin,ResolvedConfig} from 'vite';
+
+const gameRoot=resolve(import.meta.dirname,'..');
+const keys=new Set(['bgm_menu','bgm_battle','bgm_boss','ui_click','summon','shovel','treasure','merge_upgrade','merge_hero','hit_soft','boss_spawn','orange_lost','orange_gain','sprinkler','goose','lottery_tick','lottery_win','victory','defeat','dig','upgrade','legendary','orange']);
+// Keep this exact path/extension rule aligned with SoundManager's browser-side validator.
+const safeFile=(value:unknown):value is string=>typeof value==='string'&&/^(?:[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\/)*[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\.(?:mp3|ogg|wav|m4a|aac)$/i.test(value);
+const within=(base:string,target:string)=>{const child=relative(base,target);return child===''||(!child.startsWith(`..${process.platform==='win32'?'\\':'/'}`)&&child!=='..'&&!isAbsolute(child));};
+
+/** An in-game link can still redirect writes into source files; reject every component. */
+async function rejectOutputLinks(target:string):Promise<void> {
+  for(let current=resolve(target);;current=dirname(current)){
+    try{if((await lstat(current)).isSymbolicLink())throw new Error(`Audio manifest output link/junction is not allowed: ${current}`);}
+    catch(error){if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
+    if(current===dirname(current))return;
+  }
+}
+
+/** Check both lexical paths and existing ancestors so symlinks cannot escape the game. */
+async function assertGamePath(target:string):Promise<string> {
+  if(!within(gameRoot,resolve(target)))throw new Error('Audio manifest output must stay within the game directory');
+  const actualGame=await realpath(gameRoot);let ancestor=resolve(target);
+  for(;;){
+    try{
+      const actual=await realpath(ancestor);
+      if(!within(actualGame,actual))throw new Error('Audio manifest output resolves outside the game directory');
+      return actual;
+    }catch(error){
+      if((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code!=='ENOENT')throw error;
+      const parent=dirname(ancestor);if(parent===ancestor)throw error;ancestor=parent;
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+async function availableManifest(audioDir:string):Promise<Record<string,string>> {
+  try{
+    const actualDir=await realpath(audioDir),actualGame=await realpath(gameRoot);
+    if(!within(actualGame,actualDir))return {};
+    const value:unknown=JSON.parse(await readFile(join(audioDir,'manifest.json'),'utf8'));
+    if(!value||typeof value!=='object'||Array.isArray(value))return {};
+    const entries=await Promise.all(Object.entries(value).filter(([key,file])=>keys.has(key)&&safeFile(file)).map(async([key,file])=>{
+      try{
+        const path=join(audioDir,file),actual=await realpath(path);
+        if(!within(actualDir,actual)||!(await stat(path)).isFile())return null;
+        return [key,file] as const;
+      }catch{return null;}
+    }));
+    return Object.fromEntries(entries.filter((entry):entry is readonly[string,string]=>entry!==null));
+  }catch{return {};}
+}
+
+/** Remove unavailable optional audio before the browser receives its manifest. */
+export function audioManifestPlugin():Plugin {
+  let config:ResolvedConfig;
+  const assertOutput=async(target:string)=>{
+    const actual=await assertGamePath(target);
+    await rejectOutputLinks(target);
+    for(const source of [join(gameRoot,'public'),...(config.publicDir?[config.publicDir]:[])]){
+      const actualSource=await realpath(source).catch(()=>resolve(source));
+      if(within(resolve(source),resolve(target))||within(actualSource,actual))throw new Error('Audio build output must not overwrite public source files');
+    }
+  };
+  return {
+    name:'capybara-audio-manifest',
+    configResolved(resolved){config=resolved;},
+    configureServer(server){
+      server.middlewares.use(async(req,res,next)=>{
+        const path=req.url?.split('?')[0];
+        const basePath=config.base.startsWith('/')?config.base:'/';
+        if(path!=='/audio/manifest.json'&&path!==`${basePath}audio/manifest.json`){next();return;}
+        const manifest=config.publicDir?await availableManifest(join(config.publicDir,'audio')):{};
+        res.statusCode=200;res.setHeader('Content-Type','application/json; charset=utf-8');res.setHeader('Cache-Control','no-store');
+        res.end(JSON.stringify(manifest));
+      });
+    },
+    async closeBundle(){
+      if(config.command!=='build')return;
+      const audioDir=resolve(config.root,config.build.outDir,'audio'),manifestPath=join(audioDir,'manifest.json');
+      await assertOutput(audioDir);await assertOutput(manifestPath);
+      const manifest=await availableManifest(audioDir);
+      await mkdir(audioDir,{recursive:true});
+      await assertOutput(audioDir);await assertOutput(manifestPath);
+      await writeFile(manifestPath,`${JSON.stringify(manifest,null,2)}\n`,'utf8');
+    },
+  };
+}

+import {copyFile,mkdir,readdir,writeFile,lstat,realpath} from 'node:fs/promises';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
+
+const projectRoot=path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),'..');
+const safeName=/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*\.png$/;
+function assertInside(root,target){
+  const relative=path.relative(root,target);
+  if(relative==='..'||relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`)||path.isAbsolute(relative))throw Error(`Sprite target is outside the game directory: ${target}`);
+}
+async function rejectLink(filename){
+  try{if((await lstat(filename)).isSymbolicLink())throw Error(`Sprite target link/junction is not allowed: ${filename}`);}
+  catch(error){if(error.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
+}
+async function checkTargetDirectory(targetDir){
+  assertInside(projectRoot,targetDir);
+  // Check every existing ancestor before mkdir can follow a directory junction.
+  for(let current=targetDir;;current=path.dirname(current)){
+    await rejectLink(current);
+    if(current===path.dirname(current))break;
+  }
+  await mkdir(targetDir,{recursive:true});
+  assertInside(await realpath(projectRoot),await realpath(targetDir));
+}
+async function pngNames(directory){
+  try{return (await readdir(directory,{withFileTypes:true})).filter(entry=>entry.isFile()&&safeName.test(entry.name)).map(entry=>entry.name).sort();}
+  catch(error){if(error.code==='ENOENT')return [];throw error;}
+}
+/** Copies optional local art without deleting an existing destination override. */
+export async function syncAssets({sourceDir=path.resolve(projectRoot,'../../design/assets/sprites'),targetDir=path.resolve(projectRoot,'public/sprites')}={}){
+  targetDir=path.resolve(targetDir);
+  await checkTargetDirectory(targetDir);
+  const source=await pngNames(sourceDir);
+  // Validate the entire write batch before replacing any PNG or manifest bytes.
+  const targets=new Set([...source,'manifest.json',...(await readdir(targetDir)).filter(name=>safeName.test(name))]);
+  for(const name of targets)await rejectLink(path.join(targetDir,name));
+  for(const name of source)await copyFile(path.join(sourceDir,name),path.join(targetDir,name));
+  const ids=(await pngNames(targetDir)).map(name=>name.slice(0,-4));
+  await writeFile(path.join(targetDir,'manifest.json'),`${JSON.stringify(ids,null,2)}\n`);
+  console.log(`Sprites: copied ${source.length}; manifest contains ${ids.length} images${source.length?'':' (optional source absent or empty)'}.`);
+  return ids;
+}
+if(process.argv[1]&&path.resolve(process.argv[1])===fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)){
+  syncAssets().catch(error=>{console.error(error);process.exitCode=1;});
+}

+export interface OpponentTuning {rankIndex:number;interval:number;openingDelay:number;reactionDelay:number;counterMerges:boolean}
+export function getOpponentTuning(rankIndex:number):OpponentTuning {
+  const rank=Number.isFinite(rankIndex)?Math.max(0,Math.min(53,Math.trunc(rankIndex))):0;
+  const tier=rank<6?0:rank<15?1:rank<30?2:rank<45?3:4;
+  return {rankIndex:rank,interval:[1.65,1.3,1,.82,.68][tier],openingDelay:[3.5,2,1,.5,.3][tier],reactionDelay:[2.5,2.3,2,1.6,1.2][tier],counterMerges:tier===4};
+}

+import type {BattleState} from './types';
+import {MergeSystem} from './MergeSystem';

+export function getMergeTargets(state:BattleState,sourceId:number):Array<{unitId:number;kind:'upgrade'|'hero';heroId?:string}> {
+  const source=state.units.find(unit=>unit.id===sourceId);
+  if(!source||source.washing)return [];
+  return state.units.flatMap(candidate=>{
+    if(candidate.id===source.id||candidate.side!==source.side||candidate.washing)return [];
+    const outcome=MergeSystem.combine(source,candidate);
+    if(!outcome.ok||!outcome.unit)return [];
+    return [{unitId:candidate.id,kind:HEROES[outcome.unit.kind]?'hero' as const:'upgrade' as const,
+      ...(HEROES[outcome.unit.kind]?{heroId:outcome.unit.kind}:{})}];
+  });
+}

+export function createOpponentSetup(rankIndex:number,random:()=>number):{tuning:OpponentTuning;loadout:BattleLoadout;identity:OpponentIdentity} {
+  const tuning=getOpponentTuning(rankIndex),rank=tuning.rankIndex,tier=rank<6?0:rank<15?1:rank<30?2:rank<45?3:4;
+  const loadout:BattleLoadout={equippedWeapons:{},ownedProps:{},equippedActiveProps:[],equippedPassiveProps:[]};
+  const heroes=shuffled(Object.keys(HERO_FAMILIES),random),used=new Set<string>();
+  for(const hero of heroes){
+    if(Object.keys(loadout.equippedWeapons).length>=[0,2,4,6,12][tier])break;
+    const index=Object.keys(loadout.equippedWeapons).length,signature=tier===4||tier===3&&index<2;
+    const candidates=Object.values(WEAPONS).filter(w=>!used.has(w.id)&&w.family===HERO_FAMILIES[hero]&&

+    if(!candidates.length)continue;
+    const weapon=candidates[Math.floor(sample(random)*candidates.length)];
+    loadout.equippedWeapons[hero]=weapon.id;used.add(weapon.id);
+  }
+  const passivePool=Object.values(PROPS).filter(p=>p.type==='passive'&&!['shared_oranges','hyper_party'].includes(p.id)&&
+    (tier===1?p.rarity<=2:tier>=3?p.rarity>=2||['auto_shovel','tall_perch','banana_peel'].includes(p.id):true));
+  const count=[0,1,2+Math.floor(sample(random)*2),4,5+Math.floor(sample(random)*2)][tier];
+  loadout.equippedPassiveProps=shuffled(passivePool,random).slice(0,count).map(p=>p.id);
+  loadout.equippedActiveProps=tier<2?[]:tier===2?[sample(random)<.5?'catnip':'milk_bottle']:['milk_bottle','catnip'];
+  for(const id of [...loadout.equippedPassiveProps,...loadout.equippedActiveProps])loadout.ownedProps[id]=tier===4?PROPS[id].maxLevel:1;
+  const near=npcRows.map((row,index)=>({row,index,rank:Math.min(53,index*4)})).filter(npc=>Math.abs(npc.rank-rank)<=2);
+  const npc=near[Math.floor(sample(random)*near.length)],[zh,en,avatarId]=npc.row;
+  const identity:OpponentIdentity={id:`pet_npc_${npc.index+1}`,name:{'zh-CN':zh,en},avatarId,rankIndex:npc.rank};
+  return {tuning,loadout,identity};

+import type {Profile} from './types';
+
+export type TutorialStep=1|2|3|'done';
+export type TutorialAction='summon'|'deploy'|'dig'|'merge'|'acknowledge'|'skip';
+/** Call only after a real command succeeds; the guide never pauses the battle. */
+export function advanceTutorial(step:TutorialStep,action:TutorialAction):TutorialStep {
+  if(step==='done'||action==='skip')return 'done';
+  if(step===1&&action==='summon')return 2;
+  if(step===2&&(action==='deploy'||action==='dig'))return 3;
+  if(step===3&&(action==='merge'||action==='acknowledge'))return 'done';
+  return step;
+}
+/** The caller persists this candidate before adopting it, making storage retries safe. */
+export function finishTutorial(profile:Profile,reward:boolean):{profile:Profile;awarded:boolean} {
+  const awarded=reward&&!profile.tutorialRewardClaimed;
+  return {profile:{...profile,tutorialCompleted:true,tutorialRewardClaimed:!!profile.tutorialRewardClaimed||awarded,coins:profile.coins+(awarded?20:0)},awarded};
+}
+export function replayTutorial(profile:Profile):Profile {
+  return {...profile,tutorialCompleted:false};
+}

 /** Optional local PNG overrides. Nothing is requested unless its id is in the manifest. */
 export class AssetRegistry {
   private readonly images = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();
+  private readonly callbacks = new Set<()=>void>();
+  private settled = false;
   readonly ready: Promise<void>;
 
-  constructor(private readonly base = `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL || '/'}sprites/`) {
-    this.ready = this.loadManifest();
+  constructor(private readonly base = `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL || './'}sprites/`) {
+    this.ready = this.loadManifest().then(()=>{
+      this.settled=true;
+      for(const callback of this.callbacks){try{callback();}catch{/* A consumer cannot prevent other refreshes. */}}
+      this.callbacks.clear();
+    });
   }
 
   get(id: string): HTMLImageElement | undefined {
+    if(!/^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(id))return undefined;
     const image = this.images.get(id);
     return image?.complete && image.naturalWidth > 0 ? image : undefined;
   }
 
+  has(id:string):boolean { return !!this.get(id); }
+  url(id:string):string|undefined { return this.has(id)?`${this.base}${id}.png`:undefined; }
+  onReady(callback:()=>void):()=>void {
+    let active=true;
+    if(this.settled)queueMicrotask(()=>{if(active)callback();});else this.callbacks.add(callback);
+    return ()=>{active=false;this.callbacks.delete(callback);};
+  }
+
   private async loadManifest(): Promise<void> {
     if (typeof Image === 'undefined') return;
     try {
       const response = await fetch(`${this.base}manifest.json`);
       if (!response.ok) return;
       const manifest: unknown = await response.json();
       if (!Array.isArray(manifest)) return;
-      await Promise.all(manifest.filter((id): id is string =>
+      await Promise.all([...new Set(manifest.filter((id): id is string =>
         typeof id === 'string' && /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(id)
-      ).map(id => new Promise<void>(resolve => {
+      ))].map(id => new Promise<void>(resolve => {
         const image = new Image();
         image.onload = () => { this.images.set(id, image); resolve(); };
         image.onerror = () => resolve();
         image.src = `${this.base}${id}.png`;
       })));
     } catch {
       // Offline, malformed, or absent optional assets never prevent the game drawing.
     }
   }
 }

+  return `<img src="${url.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}" width="${n}" height="${n}" alt="${id}" data-sticker="${id}" style="object-fit:contain"/>`;
+}
+export function avatarSticker(id:string,size=64):string {const key=Object.hasOwn(portraits,id)?id:'capybara',url=spriteUrl(`avatar_${key}`);return url?image(key,url,size,64):svg(key,portraits[key],size,64);}

       const y=motion.add?motion.targetY-70*(1-p):motion.y-24*p+150*p*p;
-      drawOrange(c,x,y,10,motion.add?-.3*(1-p):motion.direction*p*5);c.restore();
+      this.drawOrangeArt(x,y,10,motion.add?-.3*(1-p):motion.direction*p*5);c.restore();
     }
   }
 
   private drawImage(image:HTMLImageElement,width:number,height:number,x=0,y=0):void {
     const ratio=Math.min(width/image.naturalWidth,height/image.naturalHeight),w=image.naturalWidth*ratio,h=image.naturalHeight*ratio;
     this.c.drawImage(image,x-w/2,y-h/2,w,h);
   }
+
+  private drawShovel(active:boolean,gold=false):void {
+    const image=this.assets.get('shovel');if(image)this.drawImage(image,82,82);else drawShovelArt(this.c,active,gold);
+  }
+
+  private drawSprinklerOverride(event:VisualEvent):void {
+    const image=this.assets.get('sprinkler');if(!image)return;
+    const points=event.points?.length?event.points:[{col:event.col,row:event.row},{col:event.targetCol??event.col,row:event.targetRow??event.row}];
+    const progress=clamp(event.duration>0?event.age/event.duration:1,0,1),step=progress*Math.max(0,points.length-1),index=Math.min(Math.floor(step),Math.max(0,points.length-2));
+    const a=points[index],b=points[Math.min(index+1,points.length-1)],local=step-index,p=cellCenter(a.col+(b.col-a.col)*local,a.row+(b.row-a.row)*local);
+    const c=this.c;c.save();c.globalAlpha*=Math.min(1,(1-progress)*7+.15);c.translate(p.x,p.y);c.rotate(Math.atan2(b.row-a.row,b.col-a.col));this.drawImage(image,70,70,0,-2);c.restore();
+    c.save();c.globalAlpha*=Math.min(1,(1-progress)*7+.15);c.translate(p.x,p.y);c.rotate(Math.atan2(b.row-a.row,b.col-a.col));
+    for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
+      const travel=(event.age*3+i/8)%1,x=15+travel*53,y=Math.sin(i*2.7)*travel*39;
+      line(c,[[x-9,y*.83],[x,y]],'#91d3df',3*(1-travel)+1);
+    }c.restore();
+  }

+import type { BattleState, GameSettings } from '../core/types';
+import { ENEMIES } from '../config/enemies';
+import { FIRST_WAVE_PREPARATION, WAVE_DURATION } from '../config/waves';
+
+export function getMusicMode(state?:BattleState, browsing=false):'menu'|'battle'|'boss' {
+  if(!state||browsing||state.phase!=='playing')return 'menu';
+  return state.enemies.some(enemy=>enemy.boss&&enemy.hp>0)?'boss':'battle';
+}
+
+export function isDeveloperMode(search: string, settings: Partial<GameSettings>): boolean {
+  return new URLSearchParams(search).get('dev') === '1' || settings.developerMode === true;
+}
+
+export function getBossHUD(state: BattleState) {
+  const bosses = state.enemies.filter(enemy => enemy.side === 'player' && enemy.boss && enemy.hp > 0)
+    .sort((a,b) => b.progress-a.progress || a.id-b.id);
+  const boss = bosses[0];
+  if (!boss) return null;
+  const interval = ENEMIES[boss.kind].interval;
+  return {id:boss.id, kind:boss.kind, count:bosses.length, hp:Math.max(0,Math.ceil(boss.hp)), maxHp:Math.ceil(boss.maxHp),
+    fraction:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,boss.hp/boss.maxHp)), cooldown:Math.max(0,Math.ceil(boss.abilityCooldown)),
+    cooldownFraction:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,boss.abilityCooldown/interval)), warning:boss.abilityCooldown<=2};
+}
+
+export function getWaveHUD(state: BattleState) {
+  const preparing = state.wave === 1 && state.spawned === 0 && state.waveTime < FIRST_WAVE_PREPARATION;
+  const kind = getBossHUD(state) ? 'boss' : preparing ? 'preparation' : 'wave';
+  const duration = kind==='preparation' ? FIRST_WAVE_PREPARATION : WAVE_DURATION;
+  return {kind, fraction:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,state.waveTime/duration)), remaining:Math.max(0,Math.ceil(duration-state.waveTime))};
+}

+export type MusicMode='menu'|'battle'|'boss';
+export type Note={frequency:number;delay:number;duration:number;volume:number;type?:OscillatorType;slide?:number;cutoff?:number;attack?:number;noise?:boolean};
+export const tempos:Record<MusicMode,number>={menu:84,battle:108,boss:124};
+const hz=(midi:number)=>440*2**((midi-69)/12);
+const note=(midi:number,duration:number,volume:number,type:OscillatorType='sine',cutoff=2200,attack=.012):Note=>({frequency:hz(midi),delay:0,duration,volume,type,cutoff,attack});
+
+/** One eighth-note of each arrangement, with independent lead, bass/pad and percussion. */
+export function musicStep(mode:MusicMode,step:number):Note[] {
+  const eighth=30/tempos[mode],bar=Math.floor(step/8)%4,index=step%8;
+  if(mode==='menu'){
+    // Cmaj9 – Am9 – Fmaj9 – G6/9. The sevenths/ninths also live in the slow pad.
+    const chords=[[48,52,55,59,62],[45,48,52,55,59],[41,45,48,52,55],[43,47,50,52,57]];
+    const chord=chords[bar],out=[note(chord[[0,2,1,3,2,4,3,1][index]]+24,eighth*1.7,.23)];
+    if(index===0)out.push(...chord.map(midi=>note(midi,eighth*8.6,.055,'triangle',850,.23)));
+    return out;
+  }
+  const roots=[48,45,41,43],root=roots[bar];
+  const melody=mode==='battle'?[12,16,19,21,19,16,14,16]:[12,19,16,19,22,21,19,16];
+  const out=[note(root+melody[index],eighth*(mode==='boss'?.75:1.15),mode==='boss'?.21:.24,'sine',mode==='boss'?2300:1900)];
+  if(index%2===0)out.push(note(root-12+(index===6?7:0),eighth*.8,.26,'triangle',700,.008));
+  // One shared short white-noise buffer is filtered differently for each pulse.
+  out.push({frequency:1700,delay:0,duration:mode==='boss'?.07:.045,volume:index%2?.045:.075,noise:true,cutoff:mode==='boss'?2400:1500,attack:.005});
+  if(mode==='boss'&&index%4===0)out.push({frequency:115,slide:43,delay:0,duration:.17,volume:.28,type:'sine',cutoff:450,attack:.006});
+  return out;
+}

+import type { TutorialStep } from '../core/Tutorial';
+import type { BattleState } from '../core/types';
+import { STAGE } from '../config/maps';
+import { getMergeTargets } from '../core/MergeTargets';
+import { t } from '../i18n/i18n';
+import { escapeHtml as esc } from './art';
+
+/** The spotlight is purely visual: it never captures the canvas or pauses simulation. */
+export function tutorialMarkup(step:TutorialStep,state:BattleState,selectedId?:number):string {
+  if(step==='done')return '';
+  let area={x:128,y:1226,w:384,h:128};
+  if(step===2){
+    area={x:STAGE.shovelX-47,y:STAGE.shovelY-47,w:94,h:94};
+    outer:for(let row=5;row<10;row++)for(let col=0;col<8;col++){
+      if(state.grid[row][col]==='O'&&!state.units.some(unit=>unit.side==='player'&&unit.location.zone==='board'&&unit.location.col===col&&unit.location.row===row)){
+        area={x:STAGE.boardX+col*STAGE.cell,y:STAGE.boardY+row*STAGE.cell,w:STAGE.cell,h:STAGE.cell};break outer;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  if(step===3){
+    const own=state.units.filter(unit=>unit.side==='player');
+    const unit=own.find(unit=>unit.id===selectedId&&getMergeTargets(state,unit.id).length>0)??own.find(unit=>getMergeTargets(state,unit.id).length>0)??own.find(unit=>unit.id===selectedId)??own[0];
+    if(unit){
+      const location=unit.location;
+      const x=location.zone==='bench'?STAGE.benchX+location.slot*STAGE.benchStep+STAGE.benchSize/2:STAGE.boardX+(location.col+.5)*STAGE.cell;
+      const y=location.zone==='bench'?STAGE.benchY+STAGE.benchSize/2:STAGE.boardY+(location.row+.5)*STAGE.cell;
+      area={x:x-49,y:y-49,w:98,h:98};
+    }else area={x:26,y:1018,w:586,h:170};
+  }
+  return `<div class="tutorial-spotlight" style="left:${area.x/640*100}%;top:${area.y/1386*100}%;width:${area.w/640*100}%;height:${area.h/1386*100}%"></div><section class="tutorial-bubble" style="top:${step===1?70:30}%" role="status" aria-live="polite"><div><strong>${esc(t(`p1.tutorial${step}.title`))}</strong><button type="button" data-action="tutorial-skip">${esc(t('p1.tutorialSkip'))}</button></div><p>${esc(t(`p1.tutorial${step}.body`))}</p><div class="tutorial-dots" aria-hidden="true">${[1,2,3].map(n=>`<i class="${n===step?'current':''}"></i>`).join('')}</div>${step===3?`<button type="button" class="button button-teal" data-action="tutorial-ack">${esc(t('p1.tutorialAck'))}</button>`:''}</section>`;
+}

+};
+const sizeOf=(size:number,fallback:number)=>Number.isFinite(size)&&size>0?Math.max(16,Math.min(512,Math.round(size))):fallback;
+function geometry(id:string,art:string,size:number):string {
+  return `<svg viewBox="-48 -48 96 96" width="${size}" height="${size}" role="img" aria-label="${id}" data-sticker="${id}"><g stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M-39 -26 Q-42 -42 -19 -40 L19 -41 Q42 -39 40 -18 L41 22 Q38 43 17 41 L-22 42 Q-43 38 -40 18Z" fill="#fff9eb" stroke="#fffdf6" stroke-width="5"/>${art}</g></svg>`;
+}
+function image(id:string,size:number):string|undefined {
+  const url=spriteUrl(id);return url?`<img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" width="${size}" height="${size}" alt="${id}" data-sticker="${id}" style="object-fit:contain"/>`:undefined;
+}
+export function propSticker(id:string,size=48):string {
+  const key=Object.hasOwn(props,id)?id:'washer',n=sizeOf(size,48),override=image(`prop_${key}`,n);
+  return override||`<svg viewBox="-48 -48 96 96" width="${n}" height="${n}" role="img" aria-label="${key}" data-sticker="prop_${key}"><g stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M-39 -26 Q-42 -42 -19 -40 L19 -41 Q42 -39 40 -18 L41 22 Q38 43 17 41 L-22 42 Q-43 38 -40 18Z" fill="#fff9eb" stroke="#fffdf6" stroke-width="5"/>${props[key]}</g></svg>`;
+}
+export function unitSticker(kind:string,size=40):string {
+  const key=Object.hasOwn(UNIT_ICONS,kind)||Object.hasOwn(enemies,kind)?kind:'hamster',n=sizeOf(size,40),override=image(key,n);
+  if(override)return override;
+  if(Object.hasOwn(enemies,key))return geometry(key,enemies[key],n);
+  if(['hamster','squirrel','bee'].includes(key))return avatarSticker(key,n);
+  return `<span role="img" aria-label="${key}" data-sticker="${key}" style="display:inline-grid;place-items:center;width:${n}px;height:${n}px;font-size:${Math.round(n*.63)}px">${escapeHtml(UNIT_ICONS[key])}</span>`;
+}
+export function sceneSticker(id:'menu_banner'|'logo_capy'|'sprinkler'|'shovel',fallback:string,size?:number):string {
+  const n=sizeOf(size??(id==='menu_banner'?400:64),64);
+  return image(id,n)||(Object.hasOwn(tools,id)?geometry(id,tools[id],n):fallback);
+}

+function imageEnvironment(ids:unknown,fail:string[]=[]){
+  const requested:string[]=[];
+  vi.stubGlobal('fetch',vi.fn(async()=>({ok:true,json:async()=>ids})));
+  vi.stubGlobal('Image',class {
+    complete=false;naturalWidth=0;naturalHeight=256;onload?:()=>void;onerror?:()=>void;
+    set src(value:string){requested.push(value);queueMicrotask(()=>{this.complete=true;this.naturalWidth=fail.some(id=>value.endsWith(`/${id}.png`))?0:256;(this.naturalWidth?this.onload:this.onerror)?.();});}
+  });return requested;

+describe('P1 asset synchronization',()=>{
+  it.each(['png','manifest','directory','ancestor'] as const)('rejects a linked %s target before changing any linked files',async kind=>{
+    const fixture=path.resolve(`.runtime/p1-assets-link-${kind}`),sourceDir=path.join(fixture,'source'),targetDir=path.join(fixture,'target'),protectedDir=path.join(fixture,'protected');
+    expect(fixture.startsWith(path.resolve('.')+path.sep)).toBe(true);

+      await writeFile(path.join(sourceDir,'apple.png'),'new art');
+      await writeFile(path.join(protectedDir,'apple.png'),'keep png');
+      await writeFile(path.join(protectedDir,'manifest.json'),'keep manifest');
+      if(kind==='directory'||kind==='ancestor'){
+        linkPath=targetDir;await symlink(protectedDir,linkPath,'junction');
+      }else{
+        await mkdir(targetDir);linkPath=path.join(targetDir,kind==='png'?'apple.png':'manifest.json');
+        await symlink(path.join(protectedDir,kind==='png'?'apple.png':'manifest.json'),linkPath,'file');
+      }
+      expect((await lstat(linkPath)).isSymbolicLink()).toBe(true);
+      const modulePath='../scripts/sync-assets.mjs';const {syncAssets}=await import(modulePath);
+      await expect(syncAssets({sourceDir,targetDir:kind==='ancestor'?path.join(targetDir,'nested'):targetDir})).rejects.toThrow(/link|junction|outside/i);
+      expect(await readFile(path.join(protectedDir,'apple.png'),'utf8')).toBe('keep png');
+      expect(await readFile(path.join(protectedDir,'manifest.json'),'utf8')).toBe('keep manifest');
+    }finally{
+      // Remove the exact link first; every resolved fixture reference is inside the game.
+      if(linkPath)await unlink(linkPath).catch(error=>{if(error.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;});
+      await rm(fixture,{recursive:true,force:true});
+    }
+  });
+  it('copies safe lowercase PNG names, keeps destination art and produces stable sorted manifests without source',async()=>{

+    }finally{await rm(fixture,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+  });

+import {afterEach,describe,expect,it} from 'vitest';
+import {mkdtemp,mkdir,readFile,writeFile,rm,symlink} from 'node:fs/promises';
+import {resolve,join,relative,isAbsolute} from 'node:path';
+import {resolveConfig,type Plugin,type ResolvedConfig} from 'vite';
+import {audioManifestPlugin} from '../scripts/audio-manifest';
+
+const gameRoot=resolve(import.meta.dirname,'..'),fixtures:string[]=[];
+type Reply={statusCode:number;headers:Record<string,string>;body:string;next:boolean};
+type Middleware=(req:{url?:string},res:{statusCode:number;setHeader:(k:string,v:string)=>void;end:(body:string)=>void},next:()=>void)=>unknown;
+async function fixture(manifest:string|object={},outDir='release'){
+  const runtime=join(gameRoot,'.runtime');await mkdir(runtime,{recursive:true});
+  const root=await mkdtemp(join(runtime,'p1-audio-manifest-'));fixtures.push(root);
+  await mkdir(join(root,'public/audio'),{recursive:true});await mkdir(join(root,outDir,'audio'),{recursive:true});
+  const text=typeof manifest==='string'?manifest:JSON.stringify(manifest);
+  await writeFile(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'),text);
+  await writeFile(join(root,outDir,'audio/manifest.json'),text);
+  const plugin=audioManifestPlugin();
+  const config=await resolveConfig({configFile:false,root,base:'./',publicDir:'public',build:{outDir}},'build');
+  await (plugin.configResolved as (config:ResolvedConfig)=>unknown)(config);
+  return {root,plugin,config,outDir};
+}
+async function files(root:string,paths:string[]){for(const file of paths){await mkdir(resolve(root,file,'..'),{recursive:true});await writeFile(join(root,file),'audio fixture');}}
+async function middleware(plugin:Plugin){
+  let handler!:Middleware;
+  await (plugin.configureServer as (server:unknown)=>unknown)({middlewares:{use:(fn:Middleware)=>{handler=fn;}}});
+  return handler;
+}
+async function request(handler:Middleware,url:string):Promise<Reply>{
+  return new Promise((done,reject)=>{
+    const reply:Reply={statusCode:0,headers:{},body:'',next:false};
+    const response={statusCode:200,setHeader:(key:string,value:string)=>{reply.headers[key.toLowerCase()]=value;},end:(body:string)=>{reply.statusCode=response.statusCode;reply.body=body;done(reply);}};
+    Promise.resolve(handler({url},response,()=>{reply.next=true;done(reply);})).catch(reject);
+  });
+}
+async function close(plugin:Plugin){await (plugin.closeBundle as ()=>unknown)();}
+afterEach(async()=>{
+  for(const root of fixtures){const child=relative(join(gameRoot,'.runtime'),root);if(child.startsWith('..')||isAbsolute(child))throw new Error('unsafe fixture cleanup');await rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true});}
+  fixtures.length=0;
+});
+
+describe('P1 zero-404 audio manifest filtering',()=>{
+  it('serves only declared safe existing audio files with HTTP 200 and leaves public unchanged',async()=>{
+    const original={bgm_menu:'music/chill.ogg',hit_soft:'hit.wav',bgm_boss:'missing.mp3',summon:'missing.wav',unknown:'hit.wav'};
+    const {root,plugin}=await fixture(original);await files(root,['public/audio/music/chill.ogg','public/audio/hit.wav']);
+    const reply=await request(await middleware(plugin),'/audio/manifest.json?fresh=1');
+    expect(reply.statusCode).toBe(200);expect(JSON.parse(reply.body)).toEqual({bgm_menu:'music/chill.ogg',hit_soft:'hit.wav'});
+    expect(reply.headers['content-type']).toContain('application/json');expect(reply.headers['cache-control']).toBe('no-store');
+    expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual(original);
+  });
+  it.each(['{broken','null','[]','"string"'])('returns HTTP 200 empty object for invalid manifest %s',async manifest=>{

+    expect(reply.statusCode).toBe(200);expect(JSON.parse(reply.body)).toEqual({});
+    await close(plugin);expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,outDir,'audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual({});
+    expect(await readFile(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'),'utf8')).toBe(manifest);
+  });
+  it('rejects unsafe paths even when the corresponding file could exist',async()=>{

+  });
+  it('does not intercept unrelated requests',async()=>{

+  });
+  it('reads fresh public changes without restart and never requests files itself',async()=>{

+    expect(JSON.parse((await request(handle,'/audio/manifest.json')).body)).toEqual({});
+    await files(root,['public/audio/later.mp3']);expect(JSON.parse((await request(handle,'/audio/manifest.json')).body)).toEqual({bgm_menu:'later.mp3'});
+  });
+  it('filters build output using resolved root/outDir and writes only that output manifest',async()=>{
+    const original={bgm_menu:'menu.MP3',dig:'fx/dig.wav',bgm_boss:'missing.ogg'};
+    const {root,plugin,outDir}=await fixture(original,'nested/output');
+    await files(root,['public/audio/menu.MP3','nested/output/audio/menu.MP3','nested/output/audio/fx/dig.wav']);
+    await close(plugin);
+    expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,outDir,'audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual({bgm_menu:'menu.MP3',dig:'fx/dig.wav'});
+    expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual(original);
+    expect(await readFile(join(root,outDir,'audio/menu.MP3'),'utf8')).toBe('audio fixture');
+  });
+  it('keeps empty/missing manifests safe in dev and build',async()=>{
+    const {root,plugin,outDir}=await fixture();await rm(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'));await rm(join(root,outDir,'audio/manifest.json'));
+    expect(JSON.parse((await request(await middleware(plugin),'/audio/manifest.json')).body)).toEqual({});
+    await close(plugin);expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,outDir,'audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual({});
+  });
+  it('does not write when resolved output points beyond the game directory',async()=>{

+    // Deliberately use a path outside the allowed game without touching that location.

+    await expect(close(plugin)).rejects.toThrow(/outside|within|game/i);
+    expect(await readFile(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'),'utf8')).toBe('{}');
+  });
+  it('honors resolved base, custom publicDir and an absolute in-game outDir',async()=>{
+    const {root,plugin,config,outDir}=await fixture({bgm_menu:'menu.mp3'});
+    const customPublic=join(root,'custom-public');await files(root,['custom-public/audio/menu.mp3',`${outDir}/audio/menu.mp3`]);
+    await writeFile(join(customPublic,'audio/manifest.json'),'{"bgm_menu":"menu.mp3"}');
+    await (plugin.configResolved as (config:ResolvedConfig)=>unknown)({...config,base:'/garden/',publicDir:customPublic,build:{...config.build,outDir:join(root,outDir)}});
+    expect(JSON.parse((await request(await middleware(plugin),'/garden/audio/manifest.json')).body)).toEqual({bgm_menu:'menu.mp3'});
+    await close(plugin);expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,outDir,'audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual({bgm_menu:'menu.mp3'});
+  });
+  it('refuses an output directory that would overwrite the public source manifest',async()=>{
+    const original={bgm_menu:'missing.mp3'}, {root,plugin,config}=await fixture(original);
+    await (plugin.configResolved as (config:ResolvedConfig)=>unknown)({...config,build:{...config.build,outDir:'public'}});
+    await expect(close(plugin)).rejects.toThrow(/public|source/i);
+    expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root,'public/audio/manifest.json'),'utf8'))).toEqual(original);
+  });

+import {afterEach,beforeEach,describe,expect,it,vi} from 'vitest';
+import {SoundManager,type SoundCue} from '../src/ui/SoundManager';
+import {BattleEngine} from '../src/core/BattleEngine';
+
+// Only the browser boundary is doubled; assertions inspect the real manager's graph.
+class Param {
+  value=1;events:{value:number;time:number;kind:string}[]=[];
+  setValueAtTime(value:number,time:number){this.value=value;this.events.push({value,time,kind:'set'});}
+  linearRampToValueAtTime(value:number,time:number){this.value=value;this.events.push({value,time,kind:'linear'});}
+  exponentialRampToValueAtTime(value:number,time:number){this.value=value;this.events.push({value,time,kind:'exp'});}
+  setTargetAtTime(value:number,time:number){this.value=value;this.events.push({value,time,kind:'target'});}
+  cancelScheduledValues(){}
+}
+class Node {
+  target?:Node;disconnected=false;
+  connect(target:Node){this.target=target;return target;}
+  disconnect(){this.disconnected=true;}
+}
+class Gain extends Node {gain=new Param();}
+class Filter extends Node {type='lowpass';frequency=new Param();Q=new Param();}
+class Source extends Node {
+  onended:(()=>void)|null=null;started:number[]=[];stopped:number[]=[];
+  start(at=0){this.started.push(at);}
+  stop(at=0){this.stopped.push(at);}
+}
+class Osc extends Source {type='sine';frequency=new Param();}
+class BufferSource extends Source {buffer:unknown;loop=false;}
+class Host {
+  static instances:Host[]=[];currentTime=0;state='suspended';destination=new Node();sampleRate=8000;
+  gains:Gain[]=[];oscillators:Osc[]=[];filters:Filter[]=[];sources:BufferSource[]=[];closed=false;decoded:unknown[]=[];
+  constructor(){Host.instances.push(this);}
+  createGain(){const g=new Gain();this.gains.push(g);return g;}
+  createOscillator(){const o=new Osc();this.oscillators.push(o);return o;}
+  createBiquadFilter(){const f=new Filter();this.filters.push(f);return f;}
+  createBuffer(_channels:number,length:number,_rate:number){return {duration:length/8000,getChannelData:()=>new Float32Array(length)};}
+  createBufferSource(){const s=new BufferSource();this.sources.push(s);return s;}
+  async decodeAudioData(bytes:ArrayBuffer){const buffer={duration:2,bytes};this.decoded.push(buffer);return buffer;}
+  async resume(){this.state='running';}
+  async close(){this.closed=true;this.state='closed';}
+}
+const settings={musicVolume:.5,soundVolume:.75,showDamageNumbers:true};
+const managers:SoundManager[]=[];
+function manager(){const m=new SoundManager(settings);managers.push(m);return m;}
+function advance(h:Host,seconds:number){for(let t=0;t<seconds-.00001;t+=.05){h.currentTime+=.05;vi.advanceTimersByTime(50);}}
+function busOf(node:Node):Node {let out=node;while(out.target&&!(out.target instanceof Node&&out.target.target===undefined))out=out.target;return out;}
+async function flush(){for(let i=0;i<15;i++)await Promise.resolve();}
+function serve(manifest:unknown,fail=false){
+  const requests:string[]=[];
+  vi.stubGlobal('fetch',async(input:string)=>{requests.push(String(input));return {ok:!fail,json:async()=>manifest,arrayBuffer:async()=>new ArrayBuffer(8)};});
+  return requests;
+}
+beforeEach(()=>{vi.useFakeTimers();vi.stubGlobal('AudioContext',Host);serve({});});
+afterEach(()=>{for(const m of managers)m.dispose();managers.length=0;Host.instances=[];vi.restoreAllMocks();vi.unstubAllGlobals();vi.useRealTimers();});
+

+  });
+  it('crossfades mode switches on one context and one scheduler, including suspended resume',async()=>{

+  it('adopts a mode requested during suspension when the gesture resumes audio',async()=>{

+    expect(getMusicMode(engine.state,true)).toBe('menu');
+    engine.pause(true);expect(getMusicMode(engine.state)).toBe('menu');engine.pause(false);
+    boss.hp=0;expect(getMusicMode(engine.state)).toBe('battle');
+    engine.state.phase='finished';expect(getMusicMode(engine.state)).toBe('menu');
+  });
+  it('defaults to a clean view, accepts only dev=1 or the saved setting', () => {
+    expect(isDeveloperMode('', {})).toBe(false);
+    expect(isDeveloperMode('?dev=0', {})).toBe(false);
+    expect(isDeveloperMode('?dev=true', {})).toBe(false);
+    expect(isDeveloperMode('?foo=1&dev=1', {})).toBe(true);
+    expect(isDeveloperMode('', {developerMode:true})).toBe(true);
+  });
+  it('selects the nearest live player boss and excludes the other lane', () => {

+  });

+describe('P1 tutorial command integration', () => {
+  // Exercise the real command handler and tutorial progression without mounting the DOM or audio.
+  function appFor(engine:BattleEngine) {
+    const app=Object.create(GameApp.prototype) as {
+      engine:BattleEngine; tutorialStep:TutorialStep; apply(result:CommandResult):void;
+      refresh():void; notify():void; sound:{play():void};
+    };
+    Object.assign(app,{engine,tutorialStep:2,refresh:()=>{},notify:()=>{},sound:{play:()=>{}}});
+    return app;
+  }
+  it('keeps step 2 after a bench merge and empty-bench move, then advances after a real board deployment', () => {
+    const engine=new BattleEngine({seed:74,ai:false,autoWaves:false}); engine.start();
+    const app=appFor(engine);
+    const first=engine.grant('hamster').unitId!, second=engine.grant('hamster').unitId!, third=engine.grant('squirrel').unitId!;
+    app.apply(engine.move(first,{zone:'bench',slot:1}));
+    expect(engine.state.units.some(unit=>unit.id===second&&unit.level===2)).toBe(true);
+    expect(app.tutorialStep).toBe(2);
+    const benchMove=engine.move(third,{zone:'bench',slot:0});
+    expect(benchMove).toMatchObject({ok:true,key:'hint.deployed'});
+    app.apply(benchMove); expect(app.tutorialStep).toBe(2);
+    const blocked=engine.move(third,{zone:'board',col:0,row:0});
+    expect(blocked.ok).toBe(false); app.apply(blocked); expect(app.tutorialStep).toBe(2);
+    const deployed=engine.move(third,{zone:'board',col:2,row:7});
+    expect(deployed).toMatchObject({ok:true,key:'hint.deployed'});
+    app.apply(deployed); expect(app.tutorialStep).toBe(3);
+  });
+  it('advances step 2 only after digging succeeds', () => {
+    const engine=new BattleEngine({seed:75,ai:false,autoWaves:false}); engine.start();
+    const app=appFor(engine); engine.state.sides.player.shovels=0;
+    const failed=engine.dig(0,5); expect(failed.ok).toBe(false);
+    app.apply(failed); expect(app.tutorialStep).toBe(2);
+    engine.state.sides.player.shovels=1;
+    const dug=engine.dig(0,5); expect(dug).toMatchObject({ok:true,key:'hint.dig'});
+    app.apply(dug); expect(engine.state.grid[5][0]).toBe('O'); expect(app.tutorialStep).toBe(3);
+  });
+});
